- **legal-privacy-data-protection**: Use when identifying data-privacy laws for a product handling personal data. USE FOR: LGPD, GDPR, CCPA/CPRA, PIPL, PIPEDA, data-subject rights, consent, cross-border transfers, DPO, breach notification, DPIA, records of processing. DO NOT USE FOR: encryption (use security-cryptography), masking and PII storage (use security-data-protection), security frameworks (use legal-cybersecurity-compliance).
